Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in conservation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 839 and the invariant I(A,B) = 631 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 839 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the conservation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 631. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
